# Quantitative Trading Research

Autonomous research pipeline for discovering reproducible, net-of-cost trading edges.

## Cycle 857: Deterministic Drift-Flag Persistence Counter — **IMPLEMENTED AND VERIFIED; NO NEW COMPLETED SESSION**.
- UTC 2026-10-19T15:25:00Z. Monitoring + monitoring tooling; no strategy/config/cost/universe changes.
- Assigned task executed first (post-gate monitoring): no new completed US session (2026-10-19 session still open at 14:58Z). `refresh_raw_cache.py --dry-run` = no-op for all 8 symbols; `extend_paper_trading_state.py --dry-run` = "no new completed session", 0 sessions extended, 0 state writes. Canonical raw cache still pinned at 2026-10-16 (SPY 8487, QQQ 6945, IWM 6637, TLT 6094, GLD 5512, EFA 6322, EEM 5916 rows; ^VIX 451).
- **Evidence-based follow-up implemented** (defect documented in `research/reports/drift_flag_escalation_001.md` §3): the `low_rolling_sharpe` persistence count was maintained by hand and quoted inconsistently across cycles. Under the documented definition ("consecutive trading days below the 0.5 threshold") the reproducible value is **5 trading days** (2026-10-12 → 2026-10-16); the ">20 days" figure repeated in earlier entries was a calendar-day count since the first flagged session.
- New `src/evaluation/drift.py` (pure helpers: `annualized_sharpe`, `rolling_sharpe_series`, `subthreshold_streak`, `sharpe_persistence`, `subthreshold_calendar_window`) and new read-only tool `research/drift_persistence_report.py` (recomputes the count from the committed state, cross-checks it against the stored drift flag, writes nothing). The live daily update now records `persistence_days` inside the `low_rolling_sharpe` flag; the flag's firing condition is unchanged. `research/_tmp_cycle77_verify.py` recomputes the streak independently and no longer assumes the flag is present.
- Verification: pytest **42 passed in 121.62s** (27 pre-existing + 15 new offline tests in `tests/test_drift_persistence.py`); `research/drift_persistence_report.py` → recomputed rolling 63d Sharpe -0.21188351222842883 == stored drift-flag value, persistence 5 trading days, 21 of 33 sessions below threshold in the trailing 46 calendar days, exit 0; `research/_tmp_cycle77_verify.py` **OVERALL PASS** (now including the persistence section); `.github/scripts/test_kilo_supervisor_loop.py` **OK 10/10 in 0.418s**.
- Drift status unchanged: `low_rolling_sharpe` = -0.21188351222842883; decision label remains "exploratory signal degradation — monitor, do not re-optimize". New artifact: `research/reports/drift_persistence_automation_001.md`; resolution note appended to `research/reports/drift_flag_escalation_001.md` §7.
- Handoff: `.kilo-runtime/worker-disposition.json` updated (disposition verified). NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md replaced with Cycle 858: refresh the go/no-go evaluation and the deployment decision against the current 449-session paper record (the current code-level verdict is **INCONCLUSIVE**, PSR 0.6168, while `deployment_dossier_001.md` still states "GO for live deployment" from the 2026-10-09 record; the report-level rolling 63d Sharpe gate 0.9356 → -0.2119 now fails).

## Cycle 855: Session-Safe Raw-Cache Refresh Tool — **IMPLEMENTED AND VERIFIED; NO NEW COMPLETED SESSION YET**.
- UTC 2026-10-19T14:48:39Z. Tooling only; no strategy/config/cost/universe changes.
- New tool `research/refresh_raw_cache.py`: downloads the paper-trading universe (SPY, QQQ, IWM, TLT, GLD, EFA, EEM) plus `^VIX` into a scratch dir, keeps only sessions whose local close has passed (no partial bar can enter `data/raw`), appends only rows newer than the cached max date (idempotent: re-run writes 0 rows, reports "no-op"), refuses to write any bar whose session is not complete, and writes an integrity manifest (`research/reports/raw_cache_integrity_manifest.json`, per symbol: row count, max date, sha256, timestamp).
- 12 new offline unit tests in `tests/test_refresh_raw_cache.py` (no network) covering completeness filter, merge/no-op behaviour, refusal of incomplete bars, idempotency, and manifest content.
- Real run on 2026-10-19 (10:44 ET, 2026-10-19 session still open): dry-run and real run both no-op for all 8 symbols; cache pinned at last completed session 2026-10-16 (SPY 8487, QQQ 6945, IWM 6637, TLT 6094, GLD 5512, EFA 6322, EEM 5916 rows; ^VIX 451); all 8 `data/raw` parquet sha256 unchanged; manifest sha256 verified against files.
- `research/extend_paper_trading_state.py --dry-run` and real run: "no new completed session", sessions extended 0, state write count 0, state byte-identical (sha256 e291d0d3..., last_run_date 2026-10-16, NAV $133,729.22).
- Verification: `pytest -q` 27 passed in 117.52s; `research/_tmp_cycle77_verify.py` OVERALL PASS (rolling 63d Sharpe -0.21188351222842883, regime flips 6/6, 449 entries, NAV $133,729.22, max DD -4.9857%); `.github/scripts/test_kilo_supervisor_loop.py` OK.
- `.kilo-runtime/worker-disposition.json` updated (disposition verified). NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md updated for the next cycle.

## Cycle 854: Post-Extension Verification — **NO NEW COMPLETED SESSION; DATA-CACHE POLLUTION FOUND AND FIXED**.
- UTC 2026-10-19T14:32:38Z. Monitoring + tooling fix; no strategy/config/cost changes.
- Verification: pytest **15 passed in 115.80s**; research/_tmp_cycle77_verify.py **OVERALL PASS** (rolling 63d Sharpe -0.21188351222842883, date 2026-10-16, regime flips 6/6 match, run_history 449 entries, NAV $133,729.22, max drawdown -4.9857%); .github/scripts/test_kilo_supervisor_loop.py **OK 10/10 in 0.439s**.
- Extension tool runs were correct no-ops: `research/extend_paper_trading_state.py --dry-run` and the real run both reported "no new completed session / sessions extended: 0 / state write count: 0" (the 2026-10-19 US session had not closed at 10:32 ET; close is 16:00 ET). State file byte-identical before and after (sha256 `e291d0d39246d392b756ce3f79206bf5be20c9c9486880d71ff85563739ee9aa`): last_run_date 2026-10-16, 449 entries, NAV $133,729.22, drift flag `low_rolling_sharpe` = -0.21188351222842883 (5 consecutive trading days below the 0.5 threshold).
- **Defect found (Cycle 853 regression)**: the extension tool called `fetch_universe(..., force=True)` with the default `raw_dir`, so every monitoring/no-op run **rewrote 7 canonical `data/raw/*.parquet` files** and appended a *partial* 2026-10-19 bar (SPY volume 6,623,724 vs 64,035,200 on 2026-10-16) into the committed dataset that `src/backtest/engine.py` / `src/simulation/engine.py` read cache-only (`force=False`). Evidence: sha256 of SPY/QQQ/IWM/TLT/GLD/EFA/EEM parquet files changed after a single `--dry-run`.
- **Fix (tooling only)**: `research/extend_paper_trading_state.py` now (a) downloads into a scratch temp dir so `data/raw` is read-only for monitoring, (b) drops in-session/partial bars via `complete_session_view`, (c) uses a single `session_is_complete` implementation honouring `data_refresh.market_close_hour`, and (d) writes state at most once and only if the update lands exactly on the target session. Canonical cache restored to the last completed session 2026-10-16 (SPY 8487, QQQ 6945, IWM 6637, TLT 6094, GLD 5512, EFA 6322, EEM 5916 rows; ^VIX 451 rows max 2026-10-16; non-universe symbols max 2026-10-08). Two consecutive tool runs now leave all 19 parquet files and the state file byte-identical.
- **New evidence**: an offline sandbox replay (real scratch fetch + real simulation, temp copy of the state truncated to 2026-10-15) reproduced the canonical 2026-10-16 session **exactly** on all 9 fields (nav 133729.224310211, daily_return -0.011098684337182554, daily_pnl -1500.8761984308862, turnover 0.017041067986994562, vol_scale 0.631325056899184, dd_scale 1.0, regime_exposure 1.0, n_positions 4) -> the incremental write path is deterministic and matches the original daily run.
- Tests: `tests/test_paper_trading_extend.py` extended from 1 to 8 offline tests (no network) covering the session guard, configured close hour, incomplete-bar exclusion, no-op path, dry-run path, single-write + idempotency, abort-when-target-mismatch, and "canonical cache untouched by main()".
- Handoff: `.kilo-runtime/worker-disposition.json` updated (schema_version 1, recommendation_id "n/a", disposition "verified", updated_at 2026-10-19T14:32:38Z). NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md replaced with Cycle 855. Next task: build a session-safe, idempotent raw-cache refresh tool (complete sessions only) with an integrity manifest, since nothing refreshes `data/raw` any more and the canonical dataset is pinned at 2026-10-16 (evidence in NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md).

## Overview

This repository implements a disciplined quantitative research process focused on finding genuine market inefficiencies that can be captured by low-friction automated strategies. The system runs autonomous agent cycles that iterate through hypothesis formation, implementation, evaluation, and learning.

## Research Philosophy

- **Evidence over optimism**: Prioritize reproducible, net-of-cost results over impressive in-sample backtests
- **Structure over patterns**: Seek specific structural mechanisms (funding/carry, constraints, session effects, delayed discovery) rather than data-mined correlations
- **Honest accounting**: All results reported net of realistic fees, slippage, and market impact
- **Statistical rigor**: Control for multiple testing, use chronological out-of-sample evaluation, track hypothesis count

## Project Structure

```
├── AGENTS.md              # Agent cycle instructions
├── README.md              # This file
├── NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md     # Current task specification
├── PROMPT.MD              # Mission prompt
├── data/                  # Raw and processed data
│   ├── raw/               # Original data files
│   └── processed/         # Cleaned, aligned data
├── research/              # Research notebooks and scripts
│   ├── hypotheses/        # Documented hypotheses
│   ├── experiments/       # Experiment records
│   └── reports/           # Analysis reports
├── src/                   # Source code
│   ├── data/              # Data ingestion and processing
│   ├── features/          # Feature engineering
│   ├── models/            # Model definitions
│   ├── backtest/          # Backtesting engine
│   └── evaluation/        # Evaluation metrics
├── tests/                 # Unit and integration tests
├── config/                # Configuration files
└── requirements.txt       # Python dependencies
```

- Live monitoring protocol defined: daily pre/post-market checklists, drift alert thresholds (regime flip, vol scale, DD scale, turnover, rolling Sharpe), monthly/quarterly validation cadence, continued paper trading shadow.
- Evidence base compiled: walk-forward (Cycle 6, 48 folds, Sharpe 0.9473), paper trading (Cycles 20-21, 444 days, 6 regime flips, Sharpe 2.0548), go/no-go evaluation (Cycle 22, all gates passed).
- Decision audit trail documented: hypothesis evolution from Cycles 1-23, falsification conditions tested and not met, independent reproduction verified.
- Risk assessment completed for live deployment with mitigations.
- Note: This system remains research/backtesting only; any live deployment occurs outside this environment.

## Running the Research Pipeline

The pipeline runs automatically via GitHub Actions every 5 minutes. Each cycle:
1. Reads the current task from `NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md`
2. Executes the bounded research task
3. Produces evidence artifacts
4. Writes the next task to `NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md`

## Key Documents

- [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) - Complete agent instructions and research standards
- [NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md](NEXT_AGENT_TASK.md) - Current cycle task specification
- [PROMPT.MD](PROMPT.MD) - Mission prompt

## Research Documentation Standards

All hypotheses and experiments follow strict documentation requirements defined in AGENTS.md to ensure reproducibility and prevent false discoveries.

## Safety

This is a research/backtesting system only. It never places live trades, accesses credentials, or modifies account settings.

- Cycle 870 (2026-10-19T18:55:00Z UTC): **One correct month-end rebalance schedule in shared code, executable from the backtest engine behind an opt-in parameter — the frozen schedule can now actually be run, and the incumbent's artifacts provably did not move.** No strategy, universe, cost model, gate threshold, Sharpe convention or candidate configuration was changed; no write to `data/` (the 9 sector parquet files are SHA-256 byte-identical before/after, `git status --porcelain data/` empty).
  - **New shared module** `src/backtest/rebalance.py` (single source of truth): `month_end_sessions(index)` (the last session of every calendar month), `build_month_end_weights(mask)` (equal-weight month-end segments, 1-session lag, no back-fill, no position before the first valid signal) and `normalize_schedule(name)` (an unknown schedule is a hard error, never a silent fallback).
  - **Definition used everywhere:** the month-end session is the **last session of the session calendar** in that month (so a month whose last calendar day is a weekend rebalances on the **Friday** session, and a month whose last calendar day is a market holiday on the last session that actually happened); the signal is formed **on** that session and the positions are effective from the **next** session (`signal_lag_days: 1`); a month without a valid signal opens no position.
  - **Opt-in engine parameter** `run_backtest(..., rebalance_schedule=None)`: `None`/`"daily"` (default) keeps the historical daily re-ranking, `"month_end"`/`"monthly"` executes the frozen schedule. The legacy `rebalance_freq` argument is now documented as **descriptive only** (it never changed a computation).
  - **Defect 1 closed:** the engine now reproduces the config-faithful research path exactly — `max |net_return(opt-in) − net_return(research path)| = 0.000e+00` (tolerance 1e-12) over 6,991 sessions, with bit-identical weight matrices. Before/after on the frozen candidate's universe: **1,088 trade days / 16.4849x turnover / Sharpe 0.471379** (daily re-rank) → **175 trade days / 2.9961x turnover / Sharpe 0.472367** (month-end). The defect was a **cost/turnover** defect, not a return defect.
  - **One implementation, not two:** `research/sectorrotation_long_sample_feasibility.py` now delegates its month-end construction to the shared module; its published numbers are unchanged (OVERALL: PASS, Sharpe 0.472367, report otherwise byte-identical).
  - **Defect 2 measured, behaviour deliberately unchanged:** the simulation engine's `"M"` branch fires on **235 of 6,991** sessions and has **no rebalance at all in 100 of the 335 months (29.9%)** — months whose last calendar day was a weekend or a market holiday — so it carries the previous month's weights through the whole month; where it fires it rebalances one session earlier than the shared schedule, so the two calendars change the portfolio on **0 common sessions**. Applied to the same lagged daily signal weights it differs on 1,872 of 6,991 sessions (137 trade days / 2.4400x turnover / Sharpe 0.462708 vs the shared 175 / 2.9961x / 0.472367). Its live path is `"daily"` (`config/simulation_candidate_001.yaml`), so no paper-trading artifact can move; adopting the shared implementation there is a separate, explicitly scoped change.
  - **Incumbent unchanged:** default-argument `run_backtest` on the incumbent's universe gives canonical Sharpe **0.5646255767978904**, max DD **-0.6224126346564325**, PSR **2.2219753177036448e-08** — bit-identical to the committed values; the code-level decision stays **NO-GO** with failed gates `['full_period_sharpe', 'rolling_63d_sharpe', 'max_drawdown', 'psr']`, and `research/reports/promotion_feasibility_backtest_001.md` is regenerated byte-identically.
  - **New artifacts:** tool `research/rebalance_schedule_audit.py` (read-only, deterministic, **OVERALL: PASS**, 12/12 checks, exit 0), report `research/reports/rebalance_schedule_001.md`, experiment record `research/experiments/rebalance_schedule_001.md`, and 8 regression tests in `tests/test_rebalance_schedule.py` (weekend month end → Friday session; 1-session lag; no position before the first valid signal; unknown schedule is a hard error; opt-in == research path; default path bit-identical to before; incumbent artifacts unchanged).
